HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000428/B003","root_001040/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ae89e7383d330364350","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:7:6\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"87:7:9\",\"root_000428\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gizliyi bilmek, saklı olanı yalnız seyretmekten öte, gerektiğinde açığa çıkarıp görünür kılan etkin bir bilgi niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000269/B001","root_000269/B002","root_000428/B001","root_000428/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2174f5d435124a6ca5d2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:7:7\",\"root_000269\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"87:7:9\",\"root_000428\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açık olan, seslendirilmiş okumayı; gizli kalan ise içeride tutulan hafızayı kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_231a34f3d79088d3446f","text":"4. **Hearing/listening variant of will links to reminder.** Lexical evidence: شيء B005/B006 listening closely variants; رعي B004 granting attentive hearing; ذكر B009 reminder in 87:9. Structural cue: after divine knowing, the text commands reminding if benefit occurs. Assigned role: the will/thing root's listening variants weakly activate a communicative reception field. Abductive move: the packet supplies listening branches but the surface شاء chiefly signals will; I infer only an exploratory auditory undertone because later reminder requires hearers. Reading change: the exception sits in a communication system where what is heard, spoken, and hidden is governed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000428/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_289a2f024311bb95e0b8","text":"2. **Hidden-to-open mechanism for revelation.** Lexical evidence: خ ف ي B003 (removing concealment), ج ه ر B001 (open voicing), ق ر ء B001/B002 in 87:6, ذ ك ر B004/B009 in 87:9. Structural cue: recitation precedes declared/hidden knowledge, and reminder follows. Assigned role: revelation moves from divine knowledge of hiddenness to public voicing. Abductive move: what is recited is a controlled disclosure of what could remain hidden. Reading change: \"He knows the declared and hidden\" becomes the epistemic source of reminder.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2ef4b61ca63794d77eab","text":"87:9-11 clarified that \"الجهر وما يخفى\" also applies socially: reminder is public, but only the one with خشية responds while the avoidant turns aside. 87:18-19 added another open/hidden axis: what is presently recited was already held in earlier written sheets.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4d513135d9fe30a3a8f0","text":"The final صحف made the declared/hidden contrast into a transmission concern: sheets can reveal, but also risk misreading, whereas divine علم fully marks what is public and concealed. 87:9-10 made hidden receptivity in the hearer important too.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_53f2a3ed1618ebf187dd","text":"3. **Visible marks and misreading guard.** Lexical evidence: ع ل م B002 (distinguishing mark), ص ح ف B005 in 87:18-19 (misreading from written sheets), ق ر ء B002 (recitation/teaching). Structural cue: the surah ends with sheets, while here knowledge includes what is manifest. Assigned role: divine knowledge functions as the perfect marker preventing confusion between declared and hidden. Abductive move: human media can misread signs; divine knowledge marks them without error. Reading change: 87:7 stabilizes the transmission chain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000831/B002","root_000832/B002","root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5ad4dfc8444bd40056cf","text":"1. **The exception invokes effective, informed volition.** The will-toward-a-thing variant of `ش ي ء/B002` includes willing with an implication of bringing about or attaining the thing; the disfigurement variant is not used here. `ع ل م/B001` immediately supplies comprehensive knowing, and 87:6 provides the protected process to which the exception attaches. Will serves as the selective causal operator, while knowledge supplies its complete informational field. I infer that the proximity makes the volition informed and effective rather than arbitrary. The exception reads as a controlled parameter of the recitation-memory process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_91eb2c63aebfc07e39a7","text":"2. **Open recitation and hidden memory are both known.** Lexical evidence: جهر B001 aloud declaration/recitation and B002 visible manifestation; خفي B002 inner/hidden things; قرء B001 recitation and نسي B001 recall-loss in 87:6. Structural cue: the open/hidden pair directly follows recitation/forgetting. Assigned role: جهر is the voiced output; خفي is the unvoiced retained or concealed layer. Abductive move: the packet supplies public voice and hidden cover; I infer memory/utterance polarity. Reading change: the ayah reads as assurance over both performance and inward retention.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000428/B001","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a2af3e733d5419f71f07","text":"3. **Manifest behavior functions as a mark of hidden orientation.** `ع ل م/B002` is a distinguishing mark that points to something, and `ج ه ر/B002` is plain manifestation. The split between the one who recollects in 87:10 and the one who avoids in 87:11 provides two visible marks, while `خ ف ي/B001` supplies the concealed disposition they index. I supply the arrow from behavior to inward state. This changes `يَعْلَمُ` from simple possession of facts into knowledge that reads outward signs together with what lies beneath them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a3a18b9b18384a394172","text":"The later split in 87:10–11 made the manifest/hidden pair behavioral rather than merely abstract: recollection and avoidance are visible signs pointing toward concealed orientation. 87:12 added a further surprise through `ن و ر/B001`: what was hidden can be exposed by the very fire encountered after avoidance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aa067a33fd386d9570d5","text":"3. **Revealing hidden things parallels pasture emergence.** Lexical evidence: خفي B003 removing concealment/bringing out; خرج B002 causing emergence in 87:4; علم B002 distinguishing mark/sign. Structural cue: earlier emergence of pasture and later emergence of reminder frame a knowledge statement. Assigned role: divine knowledge is the capacity to mark and reveal what is covered. Abductive move: the packet supplies hiddenness/revealing and emergence; I infer epistemic emergence as analogous to vegetal emergence. Reading change: \"knows the open and hidden\" becomes active disclosure, not passive awareness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000269/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ac32e05bf2d4e8396360","text":"2. **Knowledge spans vocal output and concealed uptake.** `ج ه ر/B001` covers open voicing and recitation aloud, while `خ ف ي/B001` covers hiddenness and secrecy.
